Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ecology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 716 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 880 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 716 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ecology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 880. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
